- **agent-representation-policy**: 管控 Agent 代表使用者與第三方交流時的身份揭露、代表權限、承諾邊界與互動終止， 涵蓋 GitHub Issue / Pull Request / 評論、部落格、電子郵件、論壇及其他公開或私人對外交流。 三大核心原則：(1) 代理身份透明 Identity Disclosure — 對外發言預設僅表明「自己是 AI Agent」＋模型名稱 （provider/model-id），且每一則後續評論都須重新標示，不得因該篇曾標示過而省略。 「替使用者屬名 / 代表使用者」屬額外授權行為：僅在使用者明確授權「公開其身份」時才可為之。 「替我發表」「替我執行」「替我回應」「幫我補充」等任務指示不構成此授權； 即使身份係由 git config、commit author、GitHub 帳號等間接事實得知，亦不得自行填入。 (2) 承諾與義務限制 Commitment Control — 未獲明確授權，不得承諾未來工作、後續回覆、額外資訊或修復， 亦不得替使用者開放權限或擴大其義務；獲准執行一項操作 ≠ 獲准承諾未來的行動。 (3) 互動邊界與任務終止 Interaction Boundary — 任務交付即停止主動查詢外部回應； 外在系統沒有即時性回應才是正常現象（bot 事件觸發、CI 排隊、維護者不在線）， 不應把它當成需要追蹤的狀態，亦不得在無新事實時反覆查詢並產出彙整報表。… ([bluelovers/ai-agents-profile](https://github.com/bluelovers/ai-agents-profile/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-representation-policy) · ★1)

- **agent-responsiveness-delegation**: 你给 AI 助手派了个重活，然后发现：发消息它不回、想插话只能排队、等十几分钟才回一句——等它抬头，需求早变了。根因不是模型慢，是主脑把重活放在前台跑，整个回合被占死。这个 Skill 把这套「重活外派 + 随时应答」机制做成可落地的东西：① 规则块（一页写清判级、派完立刻让出对话、工单四要素、只回收结论、自报≠事实、拍板留主脑、跨会话任务换机制）② 跨平台落地脚本 provision_agent_responsiveness.py：探测本机已有的 Agent 环境（Hermes 的 SOUL 置顶铁律块、Claude Code 的全局指令文件，这两条已实测），把规则块幂等写入（自动备份原文件、重复执行不重复写、绝不覆盖你已有内容）；其余平台（Codex / Cursor / WorkBuddy / 任意 Agent）则导出一份可直接粘贴的规则块 markdown，你粘到系统提示或自定义指令里就生效 ③ 体检脚本 audit_agent_responsiveness.py：逐项核对规则块是否存在、是否被后续改动覆盖、探测到哪些平台——没检测到就如实报「未检测到支持的平台」，不假报通过。纯标准库、零依赖、Windows/macOS/Linux 通用。触发词：AI不回话、等太久、插话卡住、助手响应慢、主脑被占住、重活外派、子代理、多智能体、响应速度、落地配置、系统提示。 ([Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/community/clawhub/a/agent-responsiveness-delegation) · ★10)

([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-bundle-size-check-failed-sourc-6942708bfb01bf43))
- **agent-s-burp-scan-import-failed-silent-e2dbdcad234c684d**: Fixes a pentest agent report missing web findings because its Burp scan import failed silently, by making the importer validate counts and surface errors. Use when Burp found web issues that never reach the agent's report. Not for Burp scans that genuinely found nothing, which import as an explicit zero. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-burp-scan-import-failed-silent-e2dbdcad234c684d))
- **agent-s-calendar-sync-created-duplicat-c0321c44e6983b97**: For sales-outreach (SDR) agents: Stop the bleeding first, then make every send idempotent. Dedupe on a stable key (lead id, not email casing), hold a distributed lock for the duration of the send job, and record the send in the same transaction as the send call. A second run with the same key must be a no-op, not a second email. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-calendar-sync-created-duplicat-c0321c44e6983b97))
- **agent-s-canary-analysis-passed-but-use-901297212ba25649**: This skill checks canary coverage when canary analysis passes but users still see errors. It covers verifying the canary received real traffic, comparing request mix against stable, and tracing where user errors actually originate. Use it before trusting any green canary verdict during an incident. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-canary-analysis-passed-but-use-901297212ba25649))
- **agent-s-canary-compared-preview-vs-pro-d189cba844a0d18f**: Fixes canary comparisons where the agent compared production against a preview with stale routes, invalidating the comparison. Use when canary results are meaningless due to route staleness. Not for canary logic bugs or genuine preview-vs-production behavior differences. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-canary-compared-preview-vs-pro-d189cba844a0d18f))
- **agent-s-candidate-dedup-dropped-the-ne-60e9a206c08e6847**: Fixes dedup that keeps stale records by preferring the newest application on merge. Use when dedup discards updated resumes. - **agent-s-card-rec-stalled-because-the-s-76821c1dd4e50789**: Fixes an agent card rec stalled because the statement grouped 30 ride-share charges into one summary line the matcher could not split: source the itemised detail from the card platform, match the summary line as a many-to-one group, and configure grouped matching. Use for corporate card recs with aggregated statement lines. Not for a personal card statement with no itemised source available. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-card-rec-stalled-because-the-s-76821c1dd4e50789))
- **agent-s-cargo-audit-fix-picked-a-yanke-3f4c66cf2273db0b**: Replaces a yanked version chosen by an automated cargo audit fix. Use it when the build fails with version X has been yanked after an agent applied an audit-suggested version. Key trigger: the error names the yanked version and the lockfile pins it. Not for: a yanked version you depend on deliberately, which needs a fork or vendor, not a different version. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-cargo-audit-fix-picked-a-yanke-3f4c66cf2273db0b))
- **agent-s-cargo-update-aggressive-broke-356f652e4051d674**: Fixes builds broken by an aggressive cargo update mode that pulled versions past the manifest's semver bounds, where a conservative update would have stayed green. Use when Cargo.lock contains versions the manifest never allowed. Key trigger: locked versions exceed the declared upper bounds. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-cargo-update-aggressive-broke-356f652e4051d674))
- **agent-s-cargo-update-hit-one-failed-to-0f51ea36cb5cc57b**: Fixes agents pinning old crate versions after one transient cargo download failure. Use when a dependency agent treats a single 'failed to download' as permanent and freezes the version. Key trigger: version pinned right after one failed download. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-cargo-update-hit-one-failed-to-0f51ea36cb5cc57b))
- **agent-s-cell-by-cell-execution-hit-the-43238610f896e57c**: Handles an agent hitting an API rate limit mid-execution: backoff, cache, and pace the agent's calls. The agent must treat quota as a budget. For agent builders. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-cell-by-cell-execution-hit-the-43238610f896e57c))
- **agent-s-cell-splitter-broke-a-multilin-996e0fa66686345d**: Fixes an agent's cell splitter breaking a multiline string across cells: split only at statement boundaries, never inside strings. For agent builders. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-cell-splitter-broke-a-multilin-996e0fa66686345d))
- **agent-s-certificate-for-update-server-1803994cba80c57a**: Fixes a patch agent sync failing because the TLS certificate for the update server (WSUS, SUP, or an internal repository) expired mid-cycle, so TLS validation kills the connection. Use when sync errors begin abruptly with certificate expiry messages. Not for a client clock problem that makes valid certificates look expired. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-certificate-for-update-server-1803994cba80c57a))
- **agent-s-certificate-renewal-check-flak-48b2a52e3626117f**: A skill for flaky certificate renewal checks: treat 'not yet issued' as timing, poll the ACME order status with backoff, verify challenge visibility from public DNS, and install only after the order reads valid. Use when renewal checks fail then succeed on retry. Triggers: 'cert not yet issued on retry', 'ACME order stuck pending'. Not for: hard ACME errors, expired certs from never running renewal, CA selection. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-certificate-renewal-check-flak-48b2a52e3626117f))
- **agent-s-chain-of-custody-log-missed-th-a52f873f90e194c3**: Fixes a pentest agent chain-of-custody log that misses a rescan which cleared a finding, by logging every scan and rescan event against the finding it affects. Use when the engagement audit trail cannot explain why a finding changed state. Not for informal exploratory scans a tester ran outside the engagement record, which should be added to the log with a note. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-chain-of-custody-log-missed-th-a52f873f90e194c3))
- **agent-s-changelog-summary-hallucinated-4563cf9a8d9d08b6**: Fixes a hallucinated changelog summary in an upgrade PR. Use it when the release-notes section cites breaking changes or versions that do not exist upstream. Key trigger: a cited release note or version cannot be found in the upstream repo. Not for: a genuinely breaking upgrade, which needs a migration plan, not a citation fix. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-changelog-summary-hallucinated-4563cf9a8d9d08b6))
- **agent-s-changelog-writer-dropped-the-b-472caa6aa634701b**: Fixes changelog generators that omit the breaking-changes section. Use when release notes lack breaking changes that exist in the commits. Not for missing release notes entirely. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-changelog-writer-dropped-the-b-472caa6aa634701b))
- **agent-s-checksum-cache-was-poisoned-by-ec3ed2749d324a7d**: Fixes agent checksum cache poisoned by a partial download: validate downloads completely before caching, key cache entries by full content hash, and evict suspect entries. Use when an agent verification reuses a bad cached checksum from an interrupted download. Not for verification reading live cache instead of restored data. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-checksum-cache-was-poisoned-by-ec3ed2749d324a7d))
- **agent-s-checksum-comparison-compared-c-1d68ad9c9c80267d**: Fixes checksum comparisons that fail every restore because one side hashes compressed bytes and the other hashes uncompressed bytes, by canonicalising the hash domain in the manifest. Use when restores verify as corrupt but open correctly. Not for genuinely corrupt backup media. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-checksum-comparison-compared-c-1d68ad9c9c80267d))
- **agent-s-ci-poll-loop-never-saw-the-run-e86b3c3f9023ca84**: Replaces a CI polling loop that never observes completion with webhooks or callbacks, plus a timeout and terminal-state handling for any polling that remains. Use when an agent's poll loop spins forever, misses the finished state, or times out the job. Not for CI systems with reliable synchronous APIs where a bounded poll with backoff is already working. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-ci-poll-loop-never-saw-the-run-e86b3c3f9023ca84))
- **agent-s-citation-links-point-to-page-n-7185f64e237e2897**: Fixes citation links that point at page numbers from the wrong document version, so every reference lands on the wrong page. Use when clicking a citation opens the right document but the wrong page, or citations are consistently off by a fixed number of pages. Key trigger: citations were generated against the draft but the reader opens the executed version. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-citation-links-point-to-page-n-7185f64e237e2897))
- **agent-s-citations-point-to-pages-from-2d70ca44812264d2**: Fixes page citations invalidated by re-OCR reflow. Use when page references are systematically off after a re-OCR pass. Covers rebuilding the page map, converting citations to stable section references, and never citing volatile page numbers. Not for citations made after the final OCR. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-citations-point-to-pages-from-2d70ca44812264d2))
- **agent-s-citations-point-to-pages-from-c7c94fa164db9a10**: Fixes page citations invalidated by re-OCR reflow. Use when page references are systematically off after a re-OCR pass. - **agent-s-clause-classifier-confidence-d-586aa41382374da1**: Fixes contract-review pipelines that silently file low-confidence indemnity clauses under a catch-all label instead of flagging them for review. Use when extraction output hides indemnity, cap, or carve-out clauses in "miscellaneous" with no warning. Key trigger: below-threshold clauses disappearing into a default bucket rather than a human review queue. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-clause-classifier-confidence-d-586aa41382374da1))
- **agent-s-clause-extraction-silently-use-b3b9be3d6091df4d**: Fixes stale-cache extraction: the counterparty re-uploaded the contract and the agent analyzed the cached old version without re-parsing. It shows how to key caches on content hashes instead of filenames, verify document identity before extraction, and invalidate on upload. Use it when re-uploaded documents produce suspiciously identical results or miss changes you know were made. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-clause-extraction-silently-use-b3b9be3d6091df4d))
- **agent-s-clause-timeline-put-amendment-60d59f59a0c81c75**: Fixes contract-review agents ordering amendments by upload date instead of effective date, so override chains apply in the wrong sequence. Use it when a clause timeline or amendment history drives extraction. Key trigger: a later-effective amendment appears to lose to an earlier one. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-clause-timeline-put-amendment-60d59f59a0c81c75))
- **agent-s-cleanup-failed-deleted-a-cell-13fb5bf1586b25e4**: Fixes an agent's cleanup deleting a cell that defined a function used later: analyze dependencies before deleting anything. - **agent-s-cleanup-script-terminated-the-f8d06984e5114840**: A playbook for making destructive cost-agent filters safe: ban prefix and substring tag matching on destructive actions, require exact tag matches plus an explicit exclusion list, always preview with a dry run, and gate deletions behind human approval. Use when a cleanup agent's tag filter matched staging and prod and terminated the wrong EC2 instances. Not for rightsizing math errors, stale billing data, or anomaly false positives. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-cleanup-script-terminated-the-f8d06984e5114840))
- **agent-s-codemod-didnt-handle-crlf-line-ea68b66a89e21f12**: How to fix a codemod that did not handle CRLF line endings, leaving every file showing as changed: reverting the line-ending churn, normalizing endings in the transform, and re-running cleanly. Use when a codemod diff is all line endings and no substance. Not for real content changes mixed with line-ending noise. - **agent-s-codemod-renamed-imports-then-r-6e1ab856e87cb89d**: Fixes a rename codemod that renames imports twice because its matcher still matches the new names. Use when re-running an import rename corrupts already-renamed files. Key trigger: the second run of the codemod reports changes when it should report zero. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-codemod-renamed-imports-then-r-6e1ab856e87cb89d))
- **agent-s-column-rename-broke-three-micr-29ffa3ecce46d5e5**: Recovery when a column rename broke microservices that were still reading the old name: restoring compatibility immediately, then doing the rename properly with dual-write and phased reader migration. - **agent-s-compliance-checklist-marked-co-01ed9368c1ea437b**: Fixes compliance checklists passing on stale evidence with freshness gates. Use when controls pass without current proof. Key trigger: passing controls with old evidence. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-compliance-checklist-marked-co-01ed9368c1ea437b))
- **agent-s-compliance-evidence-export-mis-39ee075741c6c91f**: Recovers missed compliance evidence exports with backfill and window monitoring. Use when an export misses its retention window. - **agent-s-concurrent-scans-overwrote-eac-9dbe46827fabbb90**: Fixes a pentest agent's concurrent scans overwriting each other's output files, by giving every run its own output directory and filename namespace. - **agent-s-concurrent-writes-corrupting-t-a86605b1ec98be19**: Fixes concurrent agent writers corrupting a Delta log with optimistic concurrency: catch the version-conflict error and retry the transaction with backoff instead of letting writers clobber each other. Not for single-writer corruption or storage-level faults. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-concurrent-writes-corrupting-t-a86605b1ec98be19))

- **agent-s-console-scraper-failed-on-the-e0cada32275b4579**: Handles an agent's console scraper failing on a cloud provider captcha: stop and hand to a human. Use when automation hits bot checks. - **agent-s-container-crashed-when-tmp-fil-914756a3f393d462**: Fixes agent containers crashing when temp space fills during archive extraction: extract to sized volumes, stream verification, and monitor temp use. - **agent-s-container-image-scan-oomkilled-e985360503eeef85**: A skill for container image scans killed by out-of-memory: confirm the OOMKilled event, raise the runner's memory limit, cache the vulnerability database between runs, and narrow the scan scope if still tight. Not for: scan finding real vulnerabilities, choosing a scanner, registry auth failures. - **agent-s-converted-script-failed-in-ci-9395bb858c469c26**: Fixes an agent's converted script failing in CI with a missing display dependency: notebooks import display helpers that have no place in headless scripts. - **agent-s-cost-agent-webhook-retried-the-5178249dace5c078**: Fixes non-idempotent webhook retries on terminate calls by making the handler check instance state before acting. - **agent-s-cost-and-usage-athena-query-fa-43475f309f7d951f**: Fixes an Athena HIVE_PARTITION_SCHEMA_MISMATCH on CUR data by aligning the table schema with the actual partition files. - **agent-s-cost-scan-cost-more-than-it-sa-bbbd922c68361234**: Puts a cost ceiling on the cost-optimization tooling itself: partitions Athena queries and cuts scan frequency. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-cost-scan-cost-more-than-it-sa-bbbd922c68361234))
- **agent-s-coverage-gate-passed-but-misse-d056060776fccf95**: Catches the coverage gap a total-coverage gate misses by enforcing diff coverage: every line and branch the agent's patch adds must be tested. Not for legacy files with pre-existing low coverage, which need a separate remediation plan, not a per-patch gate. - **agent-s-crawl-politeness-delay-applied-59ec7037d9b00a97**: Teaches an agent to apply crawl politeness delays per host instead of per domain so subdomains get independent rate budgets. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-crawl-politeness-delay-applied-59ec7037d9b00a97))
- **agent-s-crawler-followed-facet-paginat-84816b874648d7cf**: Contains a crawler that exploded into faceted pagination: block facet combinations, consolidate signals, and protect the crawl budget. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-crawler-followed-facet-paginat-84816b874648d7cf))
- **agent-s-credential-rotation-invalidate-2718ea96f2db7993**: Fixes backup runs broken when credential rotation invalidates the service account mid-run, by pinning the credential version at run start and rotating only in an overlap window between runs. Use when backups fail right after a rotation. Not for an account that never had the required backup permissions. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-s-credential-rotation-invalidate-2718ea96f2db7993))
- **agent-s-credential-vault-lookup-failed-eb1c45f45f82eae9**: Fixes agent runs failing when credential vault lookup fails mid-run: cache securely for the run duration, retry vault calls with backoff, and distinguish vault outage from bad credentials. - **agent-s-cross-account-role-expired-mid-0715a68b23da0a66**: Fixes a cross-account sweep that reports false clean results when roles expire by validating role sessions before scanning.
